Most models were at chance zero-shot; a few examples helped mainly the closed and largest open ones. LoRA on a few thousand labels brought open models level with specialist models. When evaluated on unseen species, the best adapted VLMs outperformed specialist models trained on the same data in identifying merge errors. The project will be publicly available upon acceptance.

</details>


### [35] [Reimagine Video Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36496)
*Yu Yuan,Yawen Lu,Guoxian Song,Kevin Duarte,Ratheesh Kalarot,Di Chang,Xijun Wang,Stanley H. Chan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出 Reimagine Video Dynamics (RVD) 框架，通过将紧凑且可编辑的“动态 token”从视觉上下文中解耦，实现对视频动态的直接编辑，同时保持原视频的外观。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频编辑方法大多聚焦于改变源视频的外观，而对视频动态（场景如何演变）的控制能力有限。为解决这一局限，作者希望将视频的动态与外观解耦，使动态可以被独立编辑。

Method: RVD 通过自监督重建学习动态 token：以首帧作为视觉上下文，渲染器必须从动态 token 中恢复原始视频，从而促使该 token 捕捉场景的演变方式而非外观。此外，作者开发了一个语言引导的动态 token 编辑器，将源动态转换为目标动态，并采用可扩展的反事实视频对数据管线与两阶段训练策略进行训练。

Result: 大量实验表明，RVD 能够实现有效的视频动态编辑、免训练的时间重映射（retiming），以及外观可控的重渲染。

Conclusion: RVD 通过解耦动态与视觉上下文，为视频动态编辑提供了新的可控范式，支持动态编辑、重定时与外观控制重渲染等多种应用。

Abstract: Most video editing methods focus on changing the appearance of the source video, while offering limited control over its dynamics. We introduce Reimagine Video Dynamics (RVD), a framework that disentangles a compact, editable dynamics token from visual context. We learn this token through self-supervised reconstruction: given the first frame as visual context, a renderer must recover the original video from the dynamics token, encouraging it to capture how the scene evolves rather than how it looks. This disentanglement allows video dynamics to be edited directly while preserving visual context. We develop a language-guided dynamics-token editor that transforms source dynamics into target dynamics, and train it with a scalable counterfactual video-pair pipeline and a two-stage training strategy. Extensive experiments show that RVD enables effective video dynamics editing, training-free retiming, and appearance-controlled re-rendering.

</details>


### [36] [Foresight at the Event Boundary: Evaluating Physical Prediction in Video World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36531)
*Estela Monserrat Arriaga Santana,Julian Rosas Scull,Ehécatl Sacamch'en Núñez Rico,Hugo Jair Escalante*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种"事件锚定"评估方法，直接在因果被隐藏的时刻考察视频世界模型能否预测后续结果，发现物理前瞻是由"触发后果、时间定位、运动实现"构成的多阶段挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频世界模型被视为物理世界的预测模型，理应能预见已发生事件的后果，但现有评估多聚焦参考相似度、物理规律一致性或判断合理性，只能间接估计预测能力，缺乏对"后果预期"的直接度量。

Method: 构建基于62段受控真实自由落体录像、124个片段（涵盖三类物体）的事件锚定评估，配有细粒度释放与撞击标注及真值轨迹；评估流程将后果产生、时间定位与物理实现三者分离，测试六个当代视频生成与世界模型，并开展15名参与者、20种条件的人类研究。

Result: Runway与Veo产生释放及后续撞击事件的比率超过93%，但常明显延迟；Cosmos-Predict-2.5与MAGI-1常保持事件前状态，几乎无可测后果。在可测下落中，合理的时间安排并不必然意味着物理一致的运动。人类预测总体偏向真实记录的未来，但也揭示出多种合理延续之间的真实歧义。

Conclusion: 物理前瞻并非单一能力，而是由"启动后果、时间锚定、实现运动"依次递进的多项挑战构成，为世界模型的因果预测评估提供了直接且可分解的新范式。

Abstract: Video world models are largely regarded as predictive models of the physical world and are therefore expected to anticipate the consequences of observed events. However, evaluation has mainly focused on reference similarity, physical-law consistency, or judgment plausibility, estimating anticipation only indirectly. We address this directly: when a release or impact has just occurred but its consequence is withheld, can a world model anticipate what should happen next? We introduce an event-anchored evaluation based on 62 controlled real-world free-fall recordings and 124 clips spanning three object types, with fine-grained release and impact annotations and ground-truth trajectories. The protocol separates consequence production, temporal placement, and physical realization. Across six contemporary video generation and world models, Runway and Veo produce release and subsequent impact events at rates above 93% but often initiate them substantially late, whereas Cosmos-Predict-2.5 and MAGI-1 frequently preserve the pre-event state and produce little or no measurable consequence. Among measurable falls, plausible timing does not necessarily imply physically consistent motion. We further conduct a 15-participant, 20-condition human study in which participants describe the expected consequence from a single event-anchored frame and draw its trajectory. Human predictions favor the recorded future in aggregate while revealing genuine ambiguity among plausible continuations. Overall, physical foresight emerges as a sequence of distinct challenges: initiating a consequence, anchoring it in time, and realizing its motion.

</details>


### [37] [SCCM: Spherically Consistent Coarse Matching for ERP Dense Feature Correspondence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36545)
*Gyeonggwan Lee,Eunsoo Im,Seunghwan Hong,Junghun Suh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文针对360°等距柱状投影（ERP）图像上密集特征匹配退化的问题，提出SCCM方法，通过在粗匹配阶段显式建模并校正拓扑、度量和面积三种畸变，显著提升了匹配性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ERP是360°图像的标准表示，稳健的ERP密集匹配支撑全景立体、视图合成和全景SLAM。但基于平面图像训练的密集匹配器在ERP上系统性退化，因为ERP图表引入了拓扑、度量和面积三种耦合畸变，而标准的粗匹配和可见性估计并未显式建模这些畸变。

Method: 作者在畸变产生的粗阶段接口进行校正：成对畸变在注意力中处理，逐像素畸变在共可见性门控中处理。具体提出SCCM（球面一致粗匹配），在图表朴素（chart-naive）的交叉注意力/对偶softmax粗匹配器上增加两个球面先验：球面位置注意力（SPA）将偏航周期RoPE（拓扑）与切平面偏置（度量）配对；面积感知共可见性（AAC）应用sigmoid前的对数面积校正（面积）。图表朴素框架作为受控参考，以区分框架替换效应与球面先验效应。

Result: 在固定粗框架下，PCK@1°从0.229提升到0.275（Matterport3D），精炼架构不变。在RoMa V1框架中实例化，使用相同冻结编码器、精炼架构和损失，SCCM优于ERP原生EDM（0.163）和ERP重训练的RoMa V1（0.198），并大幅优于透视训练的匹配器。还零样本迁移到Stanford2D3D，并在户外Holo360D训练后同样领先。

Conclusion: 显式建模并校正在粗匹配阶段产生的三种ERP畸变，能有效提升360°密集特征匹配性能，且方法具有良好迁移性和泛化能力。

Abstract: Equirectangular projection (ERP) is the standard representation for 360$^\circ$ imagery, and robust dense feature matching on ERP underpins panoramic stereo, view synthesis, and omnidirectional SLAM. Dense matchers trained on flat images degrade systematically on ERP because the chart introduces three coupled distortions -- topological, metric, and area -- that standard coarse matching and visibility estimation do not explicitly model. We show that correcting the three distortions at the coarse-stage interfaces where they arise -- pairwise distortions in attention, per-pixel distortion in covisibility gating -- improves PCK@$1^\circ$ from 0.229 to 0.275 on Matterport3D under a fixed coarse scaffold, with the refiner architecture unchanged -- our central result. Concretely, SCCM (Spherically Consistent Coarse Matching) augments a chart-naive cross-attention/dual-softmax coarse matcher with two sphere-derived priors: Spherical Positional Attention (SPA) pairs a yaw-periodic RoPE (topology) with a tangent-plane bias (metric), and Area-Aware Covisibility (AAC) applies a pre-sigmoid log-area correction (area). The chart-naive scaffold serves as a controlled reference, separating the scaffold-replacement effect from the spherical-prior effect. Instantiated in the RoMa V1 framework with the same frozen encoder, refiner architecture, and loss, SCCM also outperforms the ERP-native EDM (0.163) and an ERP-retrained RoMa V1 (0.198) under a unified ERP dense matching protocol, while perspective-trained matchers largely fail on ERP. It further transfers zero-shot to Stanford2D3D and, when trained on outdoor Holo360D, leads there as well.

</details>


### [38] [How Medical VLMs Underutilize Their Vision Encoders: A Dermatology Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36557)
*Janet Wang,Yunbei Zhang,Xiao Wang,Jihun Hamm*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究医学视觉语言模型（VLM）中视觉编码器强于完整多模态模型的表现差距问题，以皮肤科为测试平台，系统分析视觉信息在端到端诊断中的使用机制，并提出结合无标签提示与低标签编码器辅助重排序的改进方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学VLM在临床图像理解上展现潜力，但存在关键性能差距：视觉编码器（如MedSigLIP）显著优于完整多模态模型（如MedGemma），平均高出10.26个百分点（零目标任务标签）。这一差距促使作者探究视觉信息在端到端诊断中如何被使用，以及为何看似合理的预测缺乏图像证据支撑。

Method: 以皮肤科为主要测试平台，系统检验三种假设；对模型内部注意力模式进行机制分析；采用"先描述后决策"（describe-then-decide）提示策略；结合无标签提示与低标签编码器辅助重排序，同时保持VLM冻结；在五个VLM骨干网络上验证干预效果，并跨其他医学模态进行表示与注意力分析。

Result: MedSigLIP编码器在零标签下平均超越MedGemma 10.26个百分点；few-shot线性探测进一步证明视觉表征的强大。机制分析显示"先描述后决策"提示策略在生成过程中将视觉注意力提升30%-40%。任务特定微调改善皮肤科分类，但在评测中降低了跨域医学问答性能。

Conclusion: 视觉编码器与完整多模态模型间存在显著性能差距，且模型预测可能缺乏图像证据支撑。通过在VLM冻结条件下组合无标签提示与低标签编码器辅助重排序，可有效缓解该问题，并在多个医学模态中得到表示与注意力分析的支持。

Abstract: Medical Vision-Language Models (VLMs) show significant promise for clinical image understanding, offering accurate diagnosis with interpretable reasoning. However, a critical performance gap exists between their strong vision encoders and the full multimodal model: in dermatology, the MedSigLIP encoder outperforms MedGemma by an average of 10.26 percentage points even when both use zero target-task labels; few-shot linear probing provides further evidence of strong visual representations. This gap motivates an investigation of how visual information is used in end-to-end diagnosis and why plausible-sounding predictions can lack grounding in image evidence. Using dermatology as our primary testbed, we systematically investigate three hypotheses for this phenomenon. We further provide a mechanistic analysis of the model's internal attention patterns, showing that a simple describe-then-decide prompting strategy increases vision attention by 30-40% during generation. Task-specific fine-tuning improves dermatology classification but reduces cross-domain medical question-answering performance in our evaluation. To address these challenges, we combine label-free prompting with low-label encoder-assisted reranking while keeping the VLM frozen. We validate the interventions across five VLM backbones in dermatology and provide supporting representation and attention analyses across additional medical modalities.

</details>


### [39] [FM-ReID: Selective Competitive Token Routing for Object Re-Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36560)
*Zhiqi Li,Xiaowei Zhou,Zeyuan Sun,Feng Gao,Junyu Dong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FM-ReID 将局部表征学习建模为竞争性 token 路由，通过 Competitive Fine-grained Mining 模块在 DINOv3 密集 token 上挖掘细粒度线索，并在动物、行人、车辆 ReID 基准上取得优异结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物体重识别中，不同身份可能拥有高度相似的全局外观，而区分它们的线索往往是局部的、异质的，并且只在特定视角下可见（如动物的斑纹、轮廓与疤痕，行人的服饰与配件细节，车辆的局部外观细节）。虽然视觉基础模型在密集 token 中编码了这些信息，但单一的整体描述子会掩盖具有判别力的局部信号。

Method: 提出 FM-ReID 端到端框架，将局部表征学习转化为选择性竞争性 token 路由。其 Competitive Fine-grained Mining 模块使用多个 mining query 和一个 residual query 竞争 DINOv3 密集 token；Above-prior selection 保留优先分配给各 mining query 的 token，而 residual 槽接收被排除在检索描述子之外的 token。由此得到的多查询描述子与整体表征联合训练用于检索，无需固定空间划分或等面积约束。

Result: FM-ReID 在动物、行人和车辆 ReID 基准上均取得强劲结果。

Conclusion: 竞争性 token 路由是增强整体式基础模型表征的一种有效方式。

Abstract: Object re-identification (ReID) faces a recurring challenge: different identities can share highly similar global appearances, while the cues that distinguish them are localized, heterogeneous, and visible only under particular viewpoints. This challenge arises in animal ReID through markings, contours, and scars, in person ReID through subtle clothing and accessory cues, and in vehicle ReID through localized appearance details. Although visual foundation models encode such information in dense tokens, a single holistic descriptor can obscure discriminative local signals. We propose FM-ReID, an end-to-end framework that formulates local representation learning as selective competitive token routing. Its Competitive Fine-grained Mining module uses multiple mining queries and a residual query to compete for dense DINOv3 tokens. Above-prior selection retains tokens preferentially allocated to each mining query, while the residual slot receives tokens excluded from the retrieval descriptors. The resulting multi-query descriptors are jointly trained with a holistic representation for retrieval, without fixed spatial partitions or equal-area constraints. FM-ReID achieves strong results on animal, person, and vehicle ReID benchmarks, supporting competitive token routing as an effective way to augment holistic foundation-model representations.

</details>


### [40] [ThinkingGuard: Decoding Implicit Hazards via Step-by-Step Risk Attribution in Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36562)
*Ruochen Zhang,Yao Huang,Yitong Sun,Jiahe Xie,Jin Yan,Jifan Ma,Yuanfang Guo,Xingxing Wei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文针对多模态大模型中的多模态隐式风险，提出数据集TriggerBench与训练框架，并训练出专用防护模型ThinkingGuard。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大模型日益应用于安全关键领域，但其可靠性受到多模态隐式风险威胁。这类风险由单独看无害的文本与中性的视觉实体在逻辑上组合后诱发不安全输出。现有检测方法忽视跨模态风险激活机制，导致单模态捷径学习和幻觉式合理化推理。

Method: 首先构建TriggerBench数据集（5,600个实例），通过形式化分离关键元素与触发元素，构造反事实对比样本对，消除风险残留，迫使模型进行真正的逻辑推理。在此基础上提出步骤监督的结构化推理训练框架，训练专用防护模型ThinkingGuard：受态势感知理论启发，将隐式风险识别解耦为渐进认知阶段，使用步骤奖励的蒙特卡洛树搜索探索最优推理轨迹，并通过双约束偏好对齐蒸馏到模型中。

Result: 在标准安全基准和隐式安全基准上的大量实验表明，ThinkingGuard取得了强劲的性能表现。

Conclusion: 该工作通过数据集与结构化推理训练框架，有效提升了多模态隐式风险的检测能力，为多模态大模型的安全部署提供了新思路与资源。

Abstract: While Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) are increasingly deployed in safety-critical domains, their reliability is threatened by multimodal implicit risks. Unlike explicit threats, these hazards emerge when individually benign text and neutral visual entities logically converge to induce unsafe outputs. Current detection methods fail to address this because they overlook the underlying risk activation mechanisms that govern cross-modal risk activation, leading to single-modality shortcut learning and hallucinated rationalizations. To bridge this gap, we first construct TriggerBench, the first dataset explicitly modeling risk compositionality (5,600 instances). By formally isolating Key Elements and Trigger Elements to build counterfactual contrastive pairs, TriggerBench eliminates risk residues and forces models to perform genuine logical deduction rather than superficial pattern matching, which provides a rigorous foundation for both large-scale training and fine-grained evaluation. Building on this, we propose a Step-Supervised Structured Reasoning training framework and employ it to train ThinkingGuard, a specialized guard model. Inspired by Situation Awareness theory, we decouple implicit risk identification into progressive cognitive stages, and utilize a step-reward Monte Carlo Tree Search algorithm to explore optimal reasoning trajectories, which are then distilled into the model through Dual-Constraint Preference Alignment. Extensive experiments across both standard and implicit safety benchmarks demonstrate that ThinkingGuard achieves strong performance. Project resources are available at https://github.com/FroggyChen/ThinkingGuard.

</details>


### [41] [AffectReveal: Event-Grounded Emotion Recognition Beyond Visual Appearances](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36563)
*Yihao Qian,Runhao Zeng,Sicheng Zhao,Feng Liang,Hongmin Cai,Mingkui Tan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出"基于事件的情绪识别"(EGER)新任务,并构建EGER-Bench基准,提出免调优框架AffectReveal,通过构建与验证被遮蔽事件线索显著提升情绪识别性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统视觉情绪识别假设预测所需证据全部包含在观察到的图像或视频中,但相同的可见反应(如眼泪)可能因输入之外的事件而传达不同情绪(悲伤或喜悦)。作者希望解决视觉证据本身往往不足以判断情绪的问题。

Method: 形式化定义EGER任务,需恢复决定情绪的事件;构建EGER-Bench(10,052个视频、10,734张图像、11种情绪、2个源域、4种视觉设置)。提出AffectReveal免调优框架:先构建并独立验证基于证据的候选事件,围绕情绪关键因素(身份、焦点人物角色、关系、结果);再将恢复的事件与面部遮蔽后的媒体内事实做双向原子证据支持交叉校验,同时保留原始未遮蔽输入用于最终预测。

Result: 六位标注者的研究显示,事件上下文使人类识别准确率从33.96%提升至72.08%。在三种下游模型和四种输入设置下,AffectReveal平均UAR提升5.26–10.53个百分点。对三个可微调模型,它在全部12项准确率比较中让未调优模型胜过仅视觉微调的对应模型,且无需更新下游参数。

Conclusion: 仅凭语义相关性不足以解决EGER,事件的身份、焦点人物角色、关系或结果被误判会导致错误情绪推断。AffectReveal通过证据校验有效提升情绪识别表现,且无需微调下游参数。

Abstract: Visual emotion recognition commonly assumes that all evidence required for prediction is contained in the observed image or video. Yet the same visible reaction can convey different emotions depending on events beyond the input: tears, for example, may indicate grief or joy. We formulate Event-Grounded Emotion Recognition (EGER), where emotion recognition requires recovering the affect-determining event. We construct EGER-Bench, comprising 10,052 videos and 10,734 images across 11 emotions, two source domains, and four visual settings. A study with six annotators shows that event context raises human recognition accuracy from 33.96% to 72.08%, confirming that visual evidence alone is often insufficient. Semantic relevance alone does not solve EGER: a plausible event may imply the wrong emotion if its identity, focal-person role, relationship, or outcome is misinterpreted. We therefore propose AffectReveal, a tuning-free framework that first constructs and independently verifies evidence-grounded alternatives over these affect-critical factors. It then cross-checks the recovered event against face-masked in-media facts through bidirectional atomic evidence support, while retaining the original unmasked input for final prediction. Across three downstream models and four input settings, AffectReveal yields average UAR gains of 5.26--10.53 points. For three fine-tunable models, it also enables untuned models to outperform their fine-tuned visual-only counterparts in all 12 accuracy comparisons, without updating downstream parameters.

</details>


### [42] [Beyond Legibility: Benchmarking Visual Text Rendering and In-Place Editing in Unified Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36598)
*Ziying Zhang,Litao Li,Junchao Liao,Tianyi Zeng,Siyu Zhu,Long Qin,Zhenghao Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出VidScribe，一个面向视频生成中视觉文本挑战的统一诊断基准，涵盖T2V、R2V、I2V、V2V四种生成范式，包含803个人工验证样本和12轴因子空间，并配套11个共享指标与2个任务特定探针的评估套件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频可以展现逼真的运动和纹理，但视觉文本一旦崩塌就会立即失效。与普通场景内容不同，视觉文本对视频生成极为苛刻：微小的笔画损坏、时间不稳定或编辑错误都会立即破坏可读性和真实感。然而现有基准将文本视为附带内容，或使用忽略时间动态的静态OCR指标，未能充分关注这一挑战。

Method: 作者构建了VidScribe诊断基准，覆盖四种生成范式：从语言生成文本（T2V）、从参考迁移文本身份（R2V）、在动态下维持文本（I2V）以及局部文本编辑（V2V）。该基准包含803个人工验证样本，横跨一个由12个条件正交因子轴组成的因子空间，涵盖内在文本属性、物理成像条件和时间行为。为了可靠评估，作者构建了基于轨迹、带门控的评估套件，包含11个共享指标和2个任务特定探针，并在严格可测量性条件下运行，同时用其评测了11个商业与开源系统。

Result: 对11个商业和开源系统的基准测试显示：视频文本能力并非单一整体，内容识别与笔画级字形正确性相互解耦。性能呈现高度任务不对称性：I2V最可靠地维持文本，而V2V编辑是主要瓶颈。反直觉的是，性能退化集中在少数以文本为中心的结构和时间因子上，而非不利的成像条件。进一步探针表明，视觉参考提升的是字形和排版保真度而非内容准确性，局部编辑也无法在不破坏未声明源文本的情况下隔离目标文本。此外，该基准还能提供可操作的训练信号，与基准对齐的偏好优化可显著改善视觉文本生成。

Conclusion: VidScribe填补了视频生成中视觉文本评估的空白，提供了统一、可测量、覆盖多范式的诊断基准，揭示了现有系统在视频文本生成上的任务不对称性与瓶颈所在，并证明该基准可转化为有效训练信号，推动视觉文本生成能力的提升。

Abstract: A video can exhibit convincing motion and photorealism yet fail immediately when visual text collapses. Unlike generic scene content, visual text is unforgiving in video generation: minor stroke corruption, temporal instability, or editing errors instantly break legibility and realism. Existing benchmarks overlook this challenge by treating text as incidental or using static OCR metrics that ignore temporal dynamics. We introduce VidScribe, a unified diagnostic benchmark spanning four generation regimes: writing from language (T2V), transferring text identity from a reference (R2V), sustaining text under dynamics (I2V), and localized text editing (V2V). VidScribe contains 803 human-verified samples across a 12-axis conditionally orthogonal factor space covering Intrinsic Text Properties, Physical Imaging Conditions, and Temporal Behavior. For reliable evaluation, we build a track-grounded, gated suite with 11 shared metrics and 2 task-specific probes under strict measurability conditions. Benchmarking 11 commercial and open-source systems shows that video text capability is non-monolithic, with content recognition decoupled from stroke-level glyph correctness. Performance is highly task-asymmetric: I2V sustains text most reliably, whereas V2V editing is the primary bottleneck. Counter-intuitively, degradation concentrates on a small subset of text-centric structural and temporal factors rather than adverse imaging conditions. Further probes show that visual references improve glyph and typographic fidelity rather than content accuracy, while localized editing fails to isolate target text without corrupting undeclared source text. Beyond evaluation, VidScribe also provides an actionable training signal, where benchmark-aligned preference optimization measurably improves visual text generation. https://huggingface.co/datasets/Vicky0720/VidScribe.

</details>


### [43] [Scaling Video Generation for Reasoning: At What Cost?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36599)
*Weihang Guo,Xiaoyu Wu,Yifei Wang,Niloofar Mireshghallah,Lydia E. Kavraki*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文通过2x2x2魔方预测任务研究视频生成模型能否推理历史帧中的隐藏信息，并考察其计算代价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究视频生成模型规模的扩大是否能让模型从过去帧中推理隐藏信息，以及需要多少计算成本。

Method: 设计受控基准：从固定视角（三个面）预测初始已复原的2x2x2魔方的九个规定动作序列；使用模拟器提供精确真值进行评估。

Result: 模型早期学会合理立方体几何，但正确贴纸配置需更多训练；验证MSE呈近似幂律缩放，但低MSE不能可靠指示推理能力。较小自回归模型以有限算力获得更高状态准确率，较大模型需更多训练才达更高准确率。约0.1 PF-days时，70M参数模型对动作后帧可见贴纸配置预测正确率44.6%，而1B模型仅0.3%，其在3.14 PF-days达83.7%。符号状态监督使20M模型在同训练数据下帧准确率从31.1%提升至67.3%。

Conclusion: 扩展视频生成有助于推理隐藏状态，但存在计算成本权衡；学习状态变化的表征可补充单纯扩大模型规模。

Abstract: We study whether scaling video generation enables models to reason about hidden information from the past frames, and at what computational cost. Our controlled benchmark requires predicting nine prescribed moves of an initially solved 2x2x2 Rubik's Cube from a fixed view of three faces. Correct predictions require inferring how actions change hidden states, and the simulator provides exact ground truth for evaluation. Models learn plausible cube geometry early, while correct sticker configurations require substantially more training. Although validation MSE follows approximate power-law scaling, lower MSE loss does not reliably indicate downstream reasoning capabilities. Smaller autoregressive models achieve higher state accuracy with limited compute, while larger models reach higher accuracy after more training. At roughly 0.1 PF-days, the 70M-parameter model correctly predicts the visible sticker configuration in 44.6% of post-action frames, compared with 0.3% for the 1B model, which reaches 83.7% at 3.14 PF-days. Symbolic state supervision raises the 20M model's frame accuracy from 31.1% to 67.3% at the same training-data budget, suggesting that learning representations of state changes can complement scaling.

</details>


### [44] [Pixel-wise Exposure for Highly Robust In-Vehicle Remote-PPG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36607)
*Jieying Wang,Xinqi Cai,Caifeng Shan,Wenjin Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 PixExpo，一种"时间换空间"的像素级曝光框架，通过循环曝光序列采集与非迭代像素级融合，缓解高动态范围场景下 rPPG 的局部过曝/欠曝问题，并发布 MEX-Drive 数据集验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 远程光电容积脉搏波（rPPG）虽能非接触监测心率，但受硬件曝光控制范式限制：固定或自动曝光在一帧内对所有像素施加统一曝光时间。在汽车座舱等强方向性阳光的高动态范围场景中，这种空间不变的曝光约束会导致面部局部过曝或欠曝，在采集端就不可逆地破坏微弱的脉动信号，任何下游算法都无法恢复。

Method: 提出 PixExpo（Pixel-wise Exposure）框架，采用"时间换空间"策略：按预定义循环曝光计划顺序采集多帧，再进行非迭代的像素级融合。在每个像素位置，选择最接近 rPPG 目标强度的观测值，该准则旨在减少局部饱和与严重欠曝，而非优化视觉观感。方法无需修改传感器，仅需可编程的帧级曝光控制。

Result: 在新提出的 MEX-Drive 数据集（48 名参与者的真实驾驶场景）上验证：PixExpo 优于厂商默认自动曝光方法，在挑战性驾驶场景中平均绝对误差（MAE）降低 7.21 bpm（从 13.94 降至 6.73 bpm），成功率提升 37.29 个百分点（从 25.95% 升至 63.24%）。

Conclusion: PixExpo 通过像素级曝光融合从根本上缓解了高动态范围下 rPPG 信号采集的硬件瓶颈，无需改动传感器即可显著提升真实驾驶场景下的心率监测精度与成功率，为 rPPG 的实际部署提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Remote photoplethysmography (rPPG) offers a promising non-contact solution for heart rate monitoring, yet its real-world robustness is fundamentally limited by an inherent hardware limitation: existing camera exposure control paradigms, whether fixed or auto-exposure, impose a uniform exposure time across all pixels within a frame. In high-dynamic-range scenes such as automotive cabins with strong directional sunlight, this spatially invariant exposure constraint inevitably leads to localized facial overexposure or underexposure, irreversibly corrupting the subtle pulsatile signals essential for rPPG at the point of capture, a physical degradation that no downstream algorithm can recover. To overcome this bottleneck, we propose PixExpo (Pixel-wise Exposure), a "temporal-for-spatial" framework that sequentially captures frames under a predefined cyclic exposure schedule and performs non-iterative pixel-wise fusion. At each pixel location, PixExpo selects the observation closest to an rPPG-motivated target intensity. This criterion seeks to reduce local saturation and severe underexposure rather than optimize perceptual appearance. PixExpo requires no sensor modification but assumes programmable frame-level exposure control. We validate the proposed PixExpo framework using our newly introduced MEX-Drive dataset, comprising 48 participants under real-world driving conditions. Experimental results demonstrate that PixExpo outperforms manufacture-default auto-exposure methods, reducing the mean absolute error (MAE) by 7.21 bpm (from 13.94 to 6.73 bpm) and increasing the success rate by 37.29 percentage points (from 25.95% to 63.24%) across challenging driving scenarios.

</details>


### [45] [CrossTimeEdit: A Decade-Spanning Cross-View Dataset and Reward-Guided Editing for Historical Street-View Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36616)
*Hanwen Lu,Jun He,Mingjia Yang,Hao Wei,Jinhao Huang,Yi Lin,Xiang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 构建十年跨度跨视角数据集 VIGOR-his，并提出将历史街景生成重构为编辑任务的 CrossTimeEdit 模型，通过 SFT 与在线 RL 多奖励优化显著提升生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 历史街景影像记录了城市演变，但覆盖不均导致历史记录存在大量空白。生成合理的过去街景既需恢复已变化的结构，又需保留持续存在的场景内容，这构成一个具有挑战性的任务。

Method: 工作分两部分：其一，构建 VIGOR-his 数据集，包含三大洲 11 座城市、43,653 个地点级四元组，自动流水线涵盖空间配对、一致性筛选、变化分类以及卫星变化描述与局部编辑指令的生成与验证。其二，提出 CrossTimeEdit，将历史街景生成重构为编辑任务：以近期街景约束视角与不变外观，以时序卫星差异作为变化证据；基于 FLUX.2 [Klein] 4B，经 SFT 后接在线 RL 训练。设计 IA、BP、QP 三项街景编辑准则作为 RL 奖励维度与评估指标，采用 Flow-GRPO 结合 GDPO 在聚合前对每个奖励维度归一化进行优化。

Result: CrossTimeEdit 在三项编辑准则上的整体性能较预训练基线提升 17.12%，并在场景一致性、视觉真实感与感知质量方面优于跨视角生成模型。

Conclusion: 所提数据集与模型为历史街景生成提供了有效方案，代码、数据集与模型权重已开源发布。

Abstract: Historical street-view imagery records urban evolution, but uneven coverage leaves substantial gaps in historical records. Generating plausible past appearances requires restoring changed structures while preserving persistent scene content. We construct VIGOR-his, a decade-spanning cross-view dataset containing 43,653 location-level quadruplets across 11 cities on three continents. Its automated pipeline performs spatial pairing, consistency screening, change classification, and the generation and validation of satellite-based change descriptions and local editing instructions. Based on VIGOR-his, we propose CrossTimeEdit, a model that reformulates historical street-view generation as editing, using recent street views to constrain viewpoint and unchanged appearance and temporal satellite differences as change evidence. Starting from FLUX.2 [Klein] 4B, we train CrossTimeEdit through supervised fine-tuning (SFT) followed by online reinforcement learning (RL). We design three street-view editing criteria, namely Instruction Alignment (IA), Background Preservation (BP), and Quality and Physical Plausibility (QP), as both RL reward dimensions and evaluation metrics. We optimize this multi-reward objective using Within Group Relative Policy Optimization for flow-matching models (Flow-GRPO) with Group reward-Decoupled Normalization Policy Optimization (GDPO), which normalizes each reward dimension before aggregation. CrossTimeEdit improves overall performance across the three editing criteria by 17.12\% over the pretrained baseline and outperforms cross-view generation models in scene consistency, visual realism, and perceptual quality. The implementation code, dataset, and model weights are available at https://luhanwen67.github.io/CrossTimeEdit-release/.

</details>


### [46] [Beyond Binary Preferences: Graded Preference Optimization for Limb-Motion Captioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36628)
*Yanan Wang,Tingsong Li,Kaixun Jiang,Chongyang Zhong,Chenwei Xoe,Zhaohe Liao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出FlexBench基准与GPA评分，并设计GM-DPO训练方法，减少视觉语言模型在视频描述中的人物与肢体动作幻觉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型能生成丰富视频描述，但常误判执行动作的人物或涉及的肢体，尤其跨镜头切换时；需要能区分“信息缺失”与“错误断言”的评估与训练方法。

Method: 构建FlexBench基准（3,105个镜头、18,161条评估查询，人工校验身份，逐人覆盖细粒度肢体动作与状态），采用参考派生清单自动评估完整描述；提出GPA评分（正确得分、错误或捏造扣分）；并基于该评分提出GM-DPO，对更严重动作错误赋予更强偏好边际与更大训练权重。

Result: 在三个VLM骨干上，GM-DPO在实质动作与GPA得分上优于其他偏好目标，GPA较DPO提升2.02–3.40分；在Qwen3-8B上加权幻觉率较DPO降低21.3%；同时保持长文本输出、改善镜头结构，并在三个额外多模态基准上具竞争力。

Conclusion: 通过细粒度、可分级评估与训练信号，可有效抑制视频描述中的人物与肢体动作幻觉，兼顾输出质量与通用多模态性能。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) can generate rich video captions, yet often misidentify which person performs an action or which limb is involved, particularly across camera cuts. Improving these details requires evaluation and training that distinguish missing information from incorrect assertions. We introduce FlexBench, a benchmark spanning 3,105 shots and 18,161 evaluation queries, with human-verified identities and systematic per-person coverage of fine-grained limb actions and states. Its reference-derived checklists support automated assessment of complete captions in their person and shot contexts. Our Graded Physical Alignment score (GPA) awards credit for correct content and deducts points for incorrect or fabricated actions, making these errors explicit in the aggregate score. Building on this rubric, we propose Graded Margin Direct Preference Optimization (GM-DPO), which assigns stronger preference margins and greater training weight to more severe action errors. Across three VLM backbones, GM-DPO achieves the highest substantive-action and GPA scores among the evaluated preference objectives, improving GPA over DPO by 2.02-3.40 points. On Qwen3-8B, it reduces the weighted hallucination rate by 21.3% relative to DPO. These gains accompany sustained long-form output, improved shot structure, and competitive performance on three additional multimodal benchmarks.

</details>


### [47] [OCA: ODE-Driven Cross-Attention for Image-to-Point-Cloud Registration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36644)
*Pei An,Jiaqi Yang,Yulong Wang,Siwen Quan,Liangliang Nan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出ODE驱动的交叉注意力模块（OCA），通过常微分方程建模I2P特征交互来缓解注意力模糊问题，可在现有框架中无缝集成，在多个基准上提升配准召回率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在基于学习的图像到点云（I2P）配准中，交叉注意力是核心组件。尽管现有交叉注意力机制取得进展，但注意力模糊仍是阻碍学习具有判别力的2D-3D对应关系的关键挑战。

Method: 作者重新审视交叉注意力，建立常微分方程（ODEs）来建模理想的I2P特征交互。基于该公式化，设计出ODE驱动的交叉注意力（OCA）模块，通过ODE对特征表示和注意力矩阵进行细化。OCA可无缝集成到现有I2P配准框架中。

Result: 将OCA集成到五个最先进的基线方法中，并在四个公开基准数据集上评估。实验结果显示，在标准、微调和零样本设置下，OCA分别将配准召回率提升最多5%、9%和15%。

Conclusion: OCA通过ODE建模有效缓解注意力模糊，具有良好通用性和可集成性，显著提升I2P配准性能，验证了其有效性。

Abstract: Cross-attention is a crucial component in learning-based image-to-point-cloud (I2P) registration. Although existing cross-attention mechanisms have achieved promising progress, attention ambiguity remains a fundamental challenge that hinders the learning of discriminative 2D-3D correspondences. To address this problem, we revisit cross-attention and establish ordinary differential equations (ODEs) to model the ideal I2P feature interaction. Based on this formulation, we develop an ODE-driven cross-attention (OCA) module that refines feature representations and attention matrices through ODEs. In practice, OCA can be seamlessly integrated into existing I2P registration frameworks. To validate its effectiveness, we incorporate OCA into five state-of-the-art baselines and evaluate on four public benchmark datasets. Experimental results demonstrate that OCA improves registration recall by up to 5\%, 9\%, and 15\% under the standard, fine-tuning, and zero-shot settings, respectively.

</details>


### [48] [VLM4Cluster: Benchmarking Deep Clustering In the Era of Vision-Language Pre-training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36648)
*Yuanwei Hu,Bo Peng,Yuheng Jia,Xinting Hu,Yadan Luo,Wenjie Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出VLM4Cluster基准，系统评估语言辅助图像聚类（LaIC）方法的真实进展，覆盖17种方法、20个数据集，并从鲁棒性、泛化性和效率三个维度进行分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉-语言预训练推动了语言辅助图像聚类（LaIC）的发展，但现有研究存在实验设置不一致、数据集选择不足、评估维度有限等问题，导致LaIC的真实进展不明。

Method: 构建VLM4Cluster综合基准，包含17种代表性方法（经典、深度、语言辅助），在20个数据集上评估，涵盖经典、挑战性、细粒度、大规模和分布外场景，并从对抗鲁棒性、分布偏移泛化、计算效率三个互补维度考察。

Result: LaIC在语义要求高的基准上显著提升聚类性能前沿，在分布偏移下泛化更强，效率-效果权衡更优；但在大规模和细粒度数据集上收益不稳定，语言辅助也未能系统性降低对对抗扰动的敏感性。

Conclusion: LaIC确有实质性进展但存在局限，VLM4Cluster为图像聚类提供了全面评估平台，代码已开源。

Abstract: Vision-language pre-training has reshaped image clustering, giving rise to language-assisted image clustering (LaIC), which leverages textual semantics to complement visual representations. Despite the rapid proliferation of LaIC methods, it remains unclear how much LaIC has actually advanced image clustering, as existing studies generally suffer from major limitations, including inconsistent experimental settings, inadequate dataset selection, and limited evaluation dimensions. To address this gap, we introduce VLM4Cluster, a comprehensive benchmark for image clustering in the era of pre-trained vision-language models (VLMs). VLM4Cluster implements 17 representative methods spanning classical, deep, and language-assisted image clustering, and evaluates them on 20 datasets covering classical, challenging, fine-grained, large-scale, and out-of-distribution settings. Beyond effectiveness, VLM4Cluster systematically investigates image clustering along three complementary dimensions: robustness to adversarial perturbations, generalization under distribution shifts, and computational efficiency. Our study shows that LaIC substantially advances the clustering performance frontier on many semantically demanding benchmarks, generally exhibits stronger generalization under distribution shifts, and achieves a more favorable effectiveness-efficiency trade-off. However, its gains become less consistent on large-scale and fine-grained datasets, while language assistance does not systematically reduce sensitivity to adversarial perturbations. VLM4Cluster is released at https://github.com/YuanweiHuu/VLM4Cluster.

</details>


### [49] [FocusVTC: Efficient and High-Performance Visual Text Compression with Adaptive Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36651)
*FangZhi Zhong,Xuerui Qiu,Yuqi Pan,Ya Liu,Shaowei Gu,Bo Xu,Guoqi Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FocusVTC通过自适应分辨率打破视觉文本压缩的固定分辨率权衡，在保持通用多模态能力的同时显著提升长上下文推理性能与速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型长上下文推理的计算与内存开销巨大。视觉文本压缩可缩短输入长度，但固定分辨率渲染存在压缩与性能的权衡：低DPI省token但影响可读性，高DPI则把token浪费在无关内容上。

Method: FocusVTC结合压缩的低DPI全局视图与选择性区域增强，并将增强视图融入推理过程。构建29.4K高质量REL-CoT示例，通过多分辨率REL监督微调教会模型定位相关区域，并用GRPO学习何时增强分辨率及如何使用观测结果，无需单独持续预训练阶段。

Result: 在RULER v1的72 DPI下，FocusVTC在2.9倍输入压缩（含工具观测）时得分87.4，而Glyph在3.0倍压缩下仅57.5；LongBench上56.40超过文本输入骨干55.86；MRCR宏平均提升13.91分；VTCBench宏平均51.19。MRCR延迟评估显示相对文本有2.79倍在线端到端加速。通用多模态能力保持，MMMU从65.12升至66.73，MME从2424.02升至2457.62。

Conclusion: FocusVTC通过自适应分辨率与证据定位机制，突破了视觉文本压缩的性能-压缩权衡，在多项长上下文基准上超越现有方法与文本骨干，并保持甚至提升通用多模态能力。

Abstract: Long-context reasoning in large language models incurs substantial computation and memory costs. Visual text compression (VTC) reduces input length by rendering text as images, but fixed-resolution rendering creates a compression-performance trade-off: low DPI saves tokens at the expense of legibility, whereas high DPI spends tokens on irrelevant content. We introduce FocusVTC, which breaks this trade-off through adaptive resolution while preserving general multimodal capabilities. It combines compressed low-DPI global views with selective region enhancement, integrating enhanced views into ongoing reasoning. We construct 29.4K high-quality Reasoning-Evidence Localization (REL) chain-of-thought examples (REL-CoT) that link reasoning traces to page indices and bounding boxes. Multi-resolution REL supervised fine-tuning (REL-SFT) teaches the model to localize relevant regions, and Group Relative Policy Optimization learns when to enhance resolution and how to use the resulting observations, without a separate continual-pretraining stage. At 72 DPI on RULER v1, FocusVTC scores 87.4 at $2.9\times$ input compression, including tool observations, versus 57.5 for Glyph at $3.0\times$ input compression. It surpasses its text-input backbone on LongBench (56.40 versus 55.86), improves the MRCR macro-average by 13.91 points, and achieves a 51.19 macro-average on VTCBench. The MRCR latency evaluation also shows a $2.79\times$ online end-to-end speedup over Text. General multimodal capabilities are preserved, with MMMU increasing from 65.12 to 66.73 and MME from 2424.02 to 2457.62.

</details>


### [50] [Not Every Correction Helps: Gain-Guided Continual Test-Time Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36655)
*Youjia Zhang,Huiling Liu,Soyun Choi,Jaehong Yoon,Sungeun Hong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出GAIN，一种无需反向传播的持续测试时自适应（CTTA）框架，遵循"历史提议、增益决定"原则，通过目标统计形成修正提议并用后验预测评估器估计源相对增益，从而确定样本特定的干预强度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CTTA方法多用置信度或熵评估预测可靠性，这些只反映模型对当前样本的自我确信度。而在CTTA中，积累的目标观测可为修正源预测提供补充证据，但随目标分布变化，该历史可能失准。因此关键问题不是修正与源预测差多少，而是是否应用以及以多强力度应用。

Method: 提出GAIN（Gain-Aware INtervention），一种免反向传播的CTTA框架。它维护紧凑的目标统计以形成修正提议，并构建考虑估计不确定性的后验预测评估器。由源相对增益估计提议的收益，通过高效一维优化在连续路径上确定样本特定的干预强度。增益控制的预测再在线更新目标统计，限制不可靠修正的传播，无需反向传播、样本存储或回放。

Result: 在五个基准上取得强预测性能，在持续自适应中具有有利的准确率—校准—效率权衡。例如在ImageNet-C上，GAIN达到61.9%准确率且校准接近源模型，在多样且具挑战性的持续偏移下保持稳定，且比代表性基于优化的CTTA基线快15.9倍。

Conclusion: GAIN通过增益感知的干预机制替代单纯依赖置信度/熵，实现了高效、稳定且良好校准的持续测试时自适应，兼顾性能与效率。

Abstract: Continual test-time adaptation (CTTA) adapts a source model to an unlabeled test stream whose distribution may change over time. Existing TTA methods often assess prediction reliability using confidence or entropy, which primarily reflect the model's self-certainty for the current sample. In CTTA, accumulated target observations can provide complementary evidence for correcting the source prediction, but this history may become misaligned as the target distribution changes. The key question is therefore not how much the correction differs from the source prediction, but whether and how strongly it should be applied. This paper proposes Gain-Aware INtervention (GAIN), a backpropagation-free CTTA framework guided by a simple principle: history proposes, gain decides. GAIN maintains compact target statistics to form a correction proposal and a posterior-predictive evaluator that accounts for estimation uncertainty. The resulting source-relative gain estimates the proposal's benefit and determines a sample-specific intervention strength along a continuous path through efficient one-dimensional optimization. Gain-controlled predictions then update the target statistics online, limiting the propagation of unreliable corrections, all without backpropagation, sample storage, or replay. Across five benchmarks, our method achieves strong predictive performance, with favorable accuracy--calibration--efficiency trade-offs in continual adaptation. On ImageNet-C, for example, GAIN achieves 61.9% accuracy with near-source calibration. It remains stable under diverse and challenging continual shifts while running 15.9x faster than a representative optimization-based CTTA baseline.

</details>


### [51] [You Only Reprogram Once: Rethinking Prolonged Training for Visual Reprogramming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36661)
*Zizhao Li,Mohammed Yaqoob Ansari,Xinyu Su,Jiayang Ao,Joseph West,Kourosh Khoshelham*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出YORO方法，通过一次前向遍历从冻结的响应空间中构建下游预测器，无需反向传播即可高效实现视觉重编程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉重编程虽然是参数高效的自适应方法，但即使骨干网络被冻结，视觉提示的优化仍需要经过完整模型进行数百个epoch的训练，计算成本高昂。作者因此思考是否充分利用了预训练模型已有的信息，而非急于改变模型输入。

Method: YORO通过Bayesian Discriminant Mapping (BDM)从流式类别统计中推导协方差感知的仿射映射，仅需一次前向遍历即可构建下游预测器，无需反向传播、优化器更新或重复访问训练集。YORO-FP可选的进一步细化视觉提示20个epoch；BDM也自然扩展到CLIP，将属性-提示相似度视为源响应。

Result: 在三种全数据设置下，YORO相比最强的无梯度映射方法平均准确率提升18.4%–24.4%；在16-shot CLIP上，四个骨干网络的平均准确率从71.4%提升至77.2%；YORO-FP在部分任务上进一步提升，验证集通常保留单次遍历预测器。

Conclusion: 视觉重编程的新范式应是：先读出冻结响应，仅在需要时优化输入。

Abstract: Visual reprogramming is a parameter-efficient method for adapting pretrained models, yet its training can remain computationally expensive: even with a frozen backbone, visual prompts are often optimized through the full model for hundreds of epochs. Before changing what the pretrained model sees, we ask whether we are fully using what it already tells us. We find that modeling the full source response can already yield strong downstream predictions without prompt optimization. Motivated by this observation, we introduce You Only Reprogram Once (YORO), which constructs a downstream predictor from the frozen response space in a single forward-only traversal. Its Bayesian Discriminant Mapping (BDM) derives a covariance-aware affine mapping from streaming class statistics, requiring no backpropagation, optimizer updates, or repeated visits to the training set. When further input adaptation helps, YORO-FP optionally refines the visual prompt for 20 epochs. BDM also extends naturally to CLIP by treating attribute-prompt similarities as source responses. Across three full-data settings, YORO improves average accuracy over the strongest prior gradient-free mapping by 18.4--24.4\%. On 16-shot CLIP, it raises the four-backbone average from 71.4\% to 77.2\%. YORO-FP provides further gains on selected tasks, while validation often retains the one-pass predictor. These results suggest a different default for visual reprogramming: read out the frozen response first, and optimize the input only when needed.

</details>


### [52] [ReWorld-Track: A Recursive Event World Model for Language-Guided Multi-Camera Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36677)
*Haoyang Wu,Shoudong Han,Chaoyue Li,Sijia Chen,Zhenyang Xie,Wang sihan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ReWorld-Track，一种递归事件世界模型，将关联不确定性带入未来预测，以提升语言引导的多摄像头目标跟踪中的身份连续性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言引导的多摄像头跟踪需在未观测间隙中保持目标身份，但相似候选与不确定回归会令早期关联不可靠，错误匹配会污染历史并跨摄像头传递身份错误。

Method: 将候选匹配与继续等待建模为替代目标状态，用其后验概率更新持续的递归信念，从而在连续观测中保留对替代轨迹的不确定性；该信念预测下一摄像头、到达时间与进入区域，并结合外观与语言证据引导关联；通过跨连续交接训练，学习保留对后续预测和身份决策有用的不确定性。

Result: 在CityFlowV2上达到65.19 HOTA，在MTMMC上达到45.36 HOTA，重复交接下身份连续性提升；在MTMMC上，其结构化后验更新比同规模通用更新器高0.50 HOTA，比固定时刻软关联高0.94 HOTA，下一摄像头准确率从86.03%提升至87.41%，后续目标回归的中位到达时间误差从0.78 s降至0.71 s。

Conclusion: 递归保留关联不确定性可有效改善多摄像头跟踪的预测与身份连续性，验证了事件世界模型在跨摄像头交接场景中的有效性。

Abstract: Language-guided multi-camera tracking must preserve a target identity across unobserved gaps, where similar candidates and uncertain returns can make early associations unreliable. A wrong match can corrupt the history used to predict later observations and propagate identity errors across subsequent camera handoffs. We propose ReWorld-Track, a recursive event world model that carries association uncertainty into future predictions. Candidate matches and continued waiting define alternative target states, whose posterior probabilities are used to update a persistent recurrent belief. This representation preserves uncertainty about alternative trajectories through successive observations. This belief predicts the next camera, arrival time, and entry region, while appearance and language evidence guide association. By training across successive handoffs, the model learns to retain uncertainty that remains useful for later predictions and identity decisions. ReWorld-Track achieves HOTA scores of 65.19 on CityFlowV2 and 45.36 on MTMMC, with improved identity continuity across repeated handoffs. On MTMMC, its structured posterior update gains 0.50 HOTA points over a similarly sized generic updater and 0.94 points over fixed-moment soft association, raising next-camera accuracy from 86.03% to 87.41% and reducing median arrival-time error from 0.78 s to 0.71 s for subsequent target returns.

</details>


### [53] [Reprogramming Vision-Language Models via Structured Prompt Reparameterization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36680)
*Zizhao Li,Chengyi Cai,Mohammed Yaqoob Ansari,Feng Liu,Joseph West,Kourosh Khoshelham*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出RVP，通过类内多提示聚合与类间残差修正实现视觉重编程，推理时等效为冻结骨干加线性分类器，几乎零额外开销，在11个少样本基准和4种CLIP骨干上稳定超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言模型的视觉重编程方法主要依赖类内提示聚合，未显式建模类间关系。而细粒度类别在文本嵌入空间中属性描述高度重叠、类间相关性强，判别线索存在于低方差细微成分中，因此需要在类间进行结构化建模。

Method: 提出重参数化类间视觉重编程（RVP）：在每个类内聚合多个文本提示，并在类间施加残差修正；理论上证明基于CLIP且输入无关的线性输出聚合的视觉重编程可表示为从冻结图像嵌入到下游logits的线性映射，据此设计结构化重参数化，建模共享语义成分与类特定差异。RVP仅需单个视觉提示。

Result: 在11个少样本分类基准和4种CLIP骨干上，RVP持续优于先前的视觉重编程方法，且推理效率相当或更优。

Conclusion: RVP通过类间残差修正与结构化重参数化，在推理时可等价为冻结骨干加线性分类器，几乎不增加计算开销，是一种高效且有效的视觉重编程框架。

Abstract: Visual reprogramming adapts pretrained models to downstream tasks by modifying their input and output interfaces while keeping the backbone fixed. In vision-language models, existing methods mainly rely on intra-class prompt aggregation and do not explicitly model relationships among classes. However, fine-grained categories often exhibit highly overlapping attribute descriptions and strong inter-class correlation in the text embedding space, where discriminative cues lie in subtle low-variance components. We propose Reparameterized Inter-Class Visual Reprogramming (RVP), a structured framework that aggregates multiple text prompts within each class and applies residual correction across classes. We also show that CLIP-based visual reprogramming with input-independent linear output aggregation can be expressed as a linear mapping from frozen image embeddings to downstream logits, and use this view to design a structured reparameterization that models shared semantic components and class-specific differences. RVP uses only a single visual prompt and can be reparameterized at inference into a frozen backbone followed by a linear classifier, incurring nearly zero computational overhead. Across 11 few-shot classification benchmarks and four CLIP backbones, RVP consistently improves over prior visual reprogramming methods with comparable or better inference efficiency.

</details>


### [54] [When Semantics Matter: Reliability-Aware Semantic-Rhythm Control for Co-Speech Gesture Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36685)
*Zhirui Xing,Long Ye,Kaige Li,Ziyi Xu,Ming Meng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出可靠性感知的语义-节奏控制框架，通过学习离散运动先验与双分支语义贡献估计，实现语义与节奏的动态平衡，并提升噪声环境下生成的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有共语手势生成方法过于依赖声学韵律，而对文本语义利用不足，尤其当语义标注不完整、含噪或缺失时，生成的手势虽节奏合理却难以表达预期语义。

Method: 1) 学习离散运动先验，将连续手势表示为紧凑结构化的运动编码空间；2) 设计双分支语义贡献估计机制，包含全多模态分支与仅音频分支，用分布差异作为条件信息增益来量化文本语义对预测运动的改变程度；3) 基于该估计构建可控语义-节奏目标，在内容相关片段增强语义引导、在节奏主导片段限制语义干预；4) 将背景噪声视为声学可靠性条件，引入噪声条件特征调制与有益潜在扰动提升鲁棒性。

Result: 在基准数据集上，该框架在语义表达力、节奏同步、运动多样性及鲁棒性之间取得了良好平衡，实现了可靠且可控的共语手势生成。

Conclusion: 通过可靠性感知的语义贡献估计与语义-节奏联合控制，可在语义不可靠或声学噪声条件下生成兼具语义一致性与节奏同步的手势，为可控共语手势生成提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Co-speech gesture generation aims to synthesize natural gestures that are both temporally synchronized with speech and semantically consistent with the spoken content. Although recent methods can generate rhythmically plausible motions, they often rely heavily on acoustic prosody while underutilizing textual semantics, especially when semantic annotations are incomplete, noisy, or unavailable. Consequently, the generated gestures may follow speech rhythm while failing to express the intended semantics. To address this problem, we propose a reliability-aware semantic-rhythm control framework for co-speech gesture generation. We first learn a discrete motion prior that represents continuous gestures in a compact and structured motion-code space. We then introduce a dual-branch semantic contribution estimation mechanism consisting of a full multimodal branch and an audio-only branch. Their distributional discrepancy is formulated as conditional information gain to quantify how much textual semantics changes the predicted motion. Based on this estimate, a controllable semantic-rhythm objective selectively strengthens semantic guidance in content-relevant segments while limiting unnecessary semantic intervention in rhythm-dominant segments. Furthermore, we treat background noise as an acoustic reliability condition and introduce noise-conditioned feature modulation together with beneficial latent perturbation to improve generation robustness under realistic acoustic environments. Experiments on benchmark datasets demonstrate that the proposed framework achieves a favorable balance among semantic expressiveness, rhythmic synchronization, motion diversity, and robustness, enabling reliable and controllable co-speech gesture generation.

</details>


### [55] [AESplat: Advancing Pose-Free Feed-Forward 3D Gaussian Splatting via Decoupled Appearance Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36693)
*Shiwei Ren,Zhiang Liu,Yongchun Fang,Hongwei Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AESplat 提出一种基于 SH 分析的解耦外观建模策略，用于无位姿前馈 3DGS，实现更高质量渲染。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无位姿前馈 3DGS 方法通常统一预测 SH 表示的外观属性，忽略了视角无关与视角相关外观的本质区别，导致渲染质量欠佳。

Method: 直接从输入图像无训练地推导零阶 SH 系数（视角无关基色），高阶 SH 系数由带两种高效 3D 感知归纳偏置的浅层 MLP 预测，以建模视角相关变化。

Result: 在多个数据集上显著优于 SOTA，在 RealEstate10K 上相比无位姿方法 NAS3R 提升 0.8 dB PSNR，相比需位姿方法 DepthSplat 提升 1.1 dB。

Conclusion: 解耦外观建模能有效提升无位姿前馈 3DGS 的渲染质量。

Abstract: Pose-free feed-forward 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) has demonstrated remarkable potential for generalized novel view synthesis. However, existing methods typically predict Gaussian appearance attributes represented by spherical harmonics (SH) in the same manner, overlooking the fundamental distinction between view-independent and view-dependent appearance, which results in suboptimal rendering quality. In this paper, we present AESplat, a novel and general framework for pose-free feed-forward 3DGS that introduces an effective decoupled appearance modeling strategy based on an analysis of SH, enabling higher-quality rendering. Specifically, AESplat directly derives the zeroth-order SH coefficient, which represents the base view-independent appearance component, from the input images without training. The higher-order SH coefficients are subsequently predicted by a shallow multilayer perceptron equipped with two efficient 3D-aware inductive biases to model view-dependent appearance variations. Extensive experiments across multiple datasets demonstrate that our method significantly outperforms state-of-the-art approaches, achieving a $0.8$ dB improvement in PSNR over the pose-free method NAS3R and a $1.1$ dB improvement over the pose-required method DepthSplat on the RealEstate10K dataset. Project page: https://aesplat.github.io/.

</details>


### [56] [Drag as Evidence: Motion-Grounded Latent Recomposition for Drag-Based Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36755)
*Xinyu Pu,Hongsong Wang,Jie Gui,Pan Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MoRe-Drag，一种运动引导的拖拽编辑方法，将像素空间形变作为粗运动证据注入生成采样轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代图像编辑器在语义操控与视觉合成上表现出色，但精确空间控制能力有限，因此催生了拖拽式编辑。然而现有拖拽方法难以兼顾拖拽精度与自然、合理、符合意图的生成效果。

Method: 将像素空间形变视为粗粒度运动证据并注入生成采样轨迹；在细化、修复（inpainting）与锚点区域执行区域感知的潜变量重组合；采用阶段自适应条件，逐步从运动引导的结构形成过渡到语义细化；并通过改造基于MLLM的文本编码器实现拖拽感知的指令推断，支持免指令界面。

Result: 在DragBench-SR与DragBench-DR上，MoRe-Drag相比强基线编辑器显著提升拖拽精度，在SOTA拖拽方法中取得更优的拖拽准确率，同时具备良好的语义一致性与视觉真实感。

Conclusion: MoRe-Drag通过区域感知潜变量重组合与阶段自适应条件，有效平衡了拖拽精度与生成质量，并提供免指令交互方式，代码与数据集将公开。

Abstract: Modern image editors excel at semantic manipulation and visual synthesis, yet remain limited in precise spatial control, motivating the development of drag-based editing. However, existing drag-based methods often struggle to balance drag accuracy with natural, plausible, and intent-aligned generation. We propose MoRe-Drag, a motion-grounded drag-based editing method. Our key insight is to treat pixel-space warping as coarse motion evidence, and to inject this evidence into the generative sampling trajectory. Specifically, MoRe-Drag performs region-aware latent recomposition over refinement, inpainting, and anchor regions, coupled with stage-adaptive conditioning that progressively shifts from motion-grounded structure formation to semantic refinement. We further support an instruction-free interface by adapting the MLLM-based text encoder for drag-aware instruction inference. Experiments on DragBench-SR and DragBench-DR show that MoRe-Drag substantially improves drag precision over strong base editors and achieves superior drag accuracy among SOTA drag-based methods, while delivering strong semantic consistency and visually realistic results. Code and dataset will be publicly released.

</details>


### [57] [NesTok: Nested Self-Aligned 1D Tokenizer for Autoregressive Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36756)
*Jiawei Zhang,Shuhao Liu,Rong Huang,Yuancheng Li,Zhihui Li,Xiaojun Chang,Changlin Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出NesTok嵌套自对齐框架，通过跨长度训练提升变长视觉tokenizer的表示能力，在重建与生成任务上取得优异表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 一维变长视觉tokenizer可自适应压缩，但现有基于嵌套dropout的方法未能充分利用tokenizer的表示容量，导致图像重建与生成性能欠佳。

Method: NesTok引入跨长度训练机制，在多个token长度上联合优化重建，并用全长序列引导较短序列，使短token序列逼近全长序列的重建质量。

Result: 在ImageNet上显著优于标准训练，rFID达0.98；下游图像生成在ImageNet 256×256上取得现有变长自回归方法中最优gFID为1.46。

Conclusion: NesTok通过嵌套自对齐有效提升了变长视觉tokenizer的性能，兼顾重建质量与生成质量。

Abstract: One-dimensional (1D) variable-length visual tokenizers enable adaptive compression by varying the number of tokens, allowing downstream autoregressive (AR) models to flexibly trade off generation quality against computational cost using a single tokenizer. However, existing approaches based on nested dropout often fail to fully exploit the representational capacity of the tokenizer, resulting in suboptimal performance in both image reconstruction and generation. In this work, we introduce NesTok, a nested self-alignment framework tailored to dynamic visual tokenizers. NesTok introduces cross-length training, which jointly optimizes reconstruction across token lengths while using the full-length sequence to guide shorter counterparts, enabling shorter token sequences to approach the reconstruction quality of full-length sequences. On ImageNet, NesTok improves substantially over standard training and achieves an rFID score of 0.98. On downstream image generation, it achieves the state-of-the-art gFID score of 1.46 on ImageNet 256$\times$256 among existing variable-length autoregressive image generation methods. Code will be available at https://github.com/jaiwei804/NesTok.

</details>


### [58] [FastVR: Efficient Streaming Video Restoration with One-Step Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36757)
*Xiaoxu Chen,Qin Yang,Haoran Bai,Sibin Deng,Ying Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FastVR是一个基于单步扩散模型的流式视频修复框架，通过轻量VAE、分块因果注意力、速度一致性正则与连续轨迹学习，在保证修复质量与时序一致性的同时，于单张H20 GPU上以11 FPS处理1080p视频。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散式视频修复虽能恢复逼真细节，但实际部署受两个效率瓶颈限制：VAE编解码开销大，以及扩散Transformer中全自注意力的二次计算成本。

Method: FastVR建立在单步扩散模型之上，采用流式框架；推理端结合轻量VAE与分块因果注意力以降低计算成本；训练端引入速度一致性正则化与连续轨迹学习以提升修复质量。

Result: 在单张H20 GPU上以11 FPS处理1080p视频，修复质量与时序一致性良好；在合成与真实世界基准上达到SOTA性能，且效率优于所评估的扩散基线。

Conclusion: FastVR在效率与性能上取得兼顾，验证了单步扩散加流式设计在视频修复中的实用价值，作者希望该工作能推动社区进一步发展。

Abstract: Diffusion-based video restoration recovers realistic details, but its practical deployment is limited by two efficiency bottlenecks: costly VAE encoding and decoding, and the quadratic cost of full self-attention in diffusion transformers (DiTs). This paper presents FastVR, a streaming video restoration framework built on a one-step diffusion model, which delivers strong restoration quality and temporal consistency while processing 1080p video at 11 FPS on a single H20 GPU. To improve inference efficiency, FastVR combines a lightweight VAE with chunk-wise causal attention, which substantially reduces the computational cost. During training, it further adopts velocity consistency regularization and continuous trajectory learning, which improve restoration quality. Extensive experiments show that FastVR is more efficient than the evaluated diffusion baselines while achieving state-of-the-art performance on synthetic and real-world benchmarks. We hope that this work supports further progress in the community.

</details>


### [59] [Dual-Mode Low-Rank Learner with Bridge-Prototype Ensemble for Vision-Language Class-Incremental Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36759)
*Chiyuan He,Zihuan Qiu,Fanman Meng,Chao Wang,Liangjiang Chen,Linfeng Xu,Qingbo Wu,Hongliang Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DuLBE：双模式低秩学习+桥原型集成分类器，用于无样本类增量学习


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于CLIP的类增量学习方法存在两类问题：一是反复更新跨任务共享组件导致知识覆盖；二是过度隔离新任务更新，阻碍了CLIP可迁移知识的复用，限制了可塑性。此外，基于文本或双模态的分类器设计未能有效整合视觉与文本模态的互补信息。

Method: DuLBE将双模式低秩学习与桥原型集成分类器耦合。它依据梯度需求分配两种视觉低秩更新模式，并通过梯度路由进行协调：从历史占用的视觉方向中选择紧凑可重写的共享模式以复用可迁移知识，残余模式则为任务特定变化提供低干扰通道。在此基础上，构建视觉原型与文本嵌入在单位超球面上的测地桥，并集成可靠的桥原型以弥补文本决策边界的模态鸿沟。

Result: 在多种设置下的广泛实验表明，DuLBE在取得最先进CIL性能的同时，保持了低秩微调的高参数效率。

Conclusion: DuLBE通过协调共享与残余低秩更新模式、并集成桥原型，在无样本类增量学习中兼顾了可塑性、知识复用与跨模态互补，实现了性能与参数效率的平衡。

Abstract: Benefiting from transferable visual-textual alignment, CLIP has been widely adopted for class-incremental learning (CIL). However, existing learners either repeatedly update components shared across tasks, leading to knowledge overwriting, or overly isolate new-task updates, hindering the reuse of CLIP's transferable knowledge and limiting plasticity. Moreover, the text-based or bimodal classifier designs still fail to effectively integrate complementary information from the visual and textual modalities. To address these challenges, we introduce DuLBE, which couples dual-mode low-rank learning with a bridge-prototype ensemble classifier for exemplar-free CIL. DuLBE allocates two visual low-rank update modes according to the gradient demand and uses gradient routing to coordinate them: a compact and rewritable shared mode is selected from historically occupied visual directions to reuse transferable knowledge, while residual modes provide low-interference channels for task-specific variations. Building on the resulting stable inter-modal structure, we further construct geodesic bridges between visual prototypes and text embeddings on the unit hypersphere, and ensemble reliable bridge prototypes to compensate for the modality-gap limitations of textual decision boundaries. Extensive experiments under multiple settings show that DuLBE achieves state-of-the-art CIL performance while retaining the high parameter efficiency of low-rank tuning.

</details>


### [60] [DSPO: Diversity-aware Subjective Policy Optimization for Robust Emotional Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36775)
*Cheng Ye,Weidong Chen,Bingyan Xu,Zhendong Mao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出DSPO强化学习框架，通过分布对齐的情感多样性奖励与反事实视觉干预门控，解决MLLM情感推理中硬标签监督与视觉幻觉问题，跨域性能显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态大模型强化学习方法在情感推理任务上存在严重不足：依赖确定性的硬标签监督和逐点孤立评估，与人类情感主观、连续分布的本质存在根本性差距；同时情感状态隐含于视觉线索中，比显式物理对象更抽象，加剧了MLLM的视觉幻觉，产生看似合理却缺乏依据的情感证据。

Method: 提出DSPO（多样性感知主观策略优化）强化学习框架，包含三部分：1）结合标注情感的词汇先验与图像特定上下文信息，在VAD空间构建上下文接地的情感分布先验；2）基于该先验提出分布对齐情感多样性奖励DEDR，通过留一法衡量每个候选情感在rollout中的边际贡献，奖励使预测情感集合更接近先验的候选，既保留合理主观解释又避免无约束发散；3）提出反事实视觉干预门控CVIG，遮蔽推理过程中高亮的视觉区域，利用候选概率变化降低缺乏视觉证据支持的解读权重。

Result: 大量实验表明，DSPO在多个公开基准上取得最先进性能，尤其在跨域性能上表现突出，平均跨域准确率相比EMO-R3提升+10.8%。

Conclusion: DSPO通过联合促进主观情感覆盖与视觉接地，有效缓解了硬标签监督与视觉幻觉问题，验证了分布对齐奖励与反事实视觉干预在情感推理强化学习中的有效性，为MLLM情感推理提供了新范式。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning has significantly advanced the complex reasoning capabilities of MLLMs. However, prevailing RL algorithms suffer a severe failure in emotion reasoning tasks. These methods heavily rely on deterministic hard-label supervision and point-wise isolated evaluation, creating a fundamental gap with the inherently subjective and continuously distributed nature of human emotions. Furthermore, unlike explicit physical objects, emotional states are deeply implicit within visual cues. This abstract nature exacerbates visual hallucinations in MLLMs, leading to plausible yet ungrounded emotional evidence. To address these limitations, we propose Diversity-Aware Subjective Policy Optimization (DSPO), a reinforcement learning framework that jointly promotes subjective affective coverage and visual grounding. First, we construct a context-grounded emotional distribution prior in the VAD space by combining the lexical prior of the annotated emotion with image-specific contextual information. Based on this prior, we introduce a Distribution-Aligned Emotional Diversity Reward (DEDR), which measures the leave-one-out marginal contribution of each candidate emotion within a rollout. DEDR rewards candidates whose inclusion brings the predicted affective set closer to the context-grounded prior, thereby preserving plausible subjective interpretations without encouraging unconstrained dispersion. We further develop Counterfactual Visual Intervention Gating (CVIG), which masks the visual region highlighted in the reasoning process and uses the resulting candidate-wise probability changes to reduce the weights of interpretations unsupported by visual evidence. Extensive experiments demonstrate that DSPO achieves state-of-the-art performance across multiple public benchmarks, especially on the cross-domain performance, i.e., improving +10.8\% on average cross-domain accuracy than EMO-R3.

</details>


### [61] [Causal-EVC: Breaking Emotional Spurious Causality via Spatiotemporal Grounding and Counterfactual Intervention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36776)
*Cheng Ye,Weidong Chen,Peipei Song,Zhendong Mao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文针对情感视频描述中视觉因果关系被简单注意力匹配导致的因果冗余与虚假关联问题，构建了带稠密时空因果标注的基准 EVC-CauseGround（含因果忠实性子集），并提出 Causal-EVC 框架，通过运动引导因果时空定位、可解释稀疏情感路由与反事实对比目标，使情感预测锚定真实因果触发因素。实验表明其在语义指标和因果忠实性上均取得最优表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有情感视频描述方法虽已意识到视觉因果对情感感知与描述生成的重要性，但基本依赖简单的注意力匹配，在共现偏置下不可避免地产生因果冗余与虚假关联（例如在阳光海滩上误将"悲伤"识别为"快乐"），从而从混淆背景中产生严重的捷径学习。此外，现有评测无法验证模型是否真正掌握因果推理，还是仅仅利用了背景混淆因素。

Method: 首先构建 EVC-CauseGround 基准，包含稠密时空因果标注，并引入精心挑选的因果忠实地子集以显式量化真实的情感-因果归因。其次提出 Causal-EVC 情感定位描述框架：引入运动引导的因果时空定位模块，将因果触发因素与背景混淆因素精确解耦；引入可解释稀疏情感路由模块；通过合成反事实表示并设计新的反事实对比目标，强制模型将情感预测严格锚定在真实因果触发因素上，而非混淆背景。

Result: 大量实验显示，Causal-EVC 不仅在语义指标上取得最佳性能，而且在因果忠实地子集上展现出显著优势。

Conclusion: 该模型能够从真实视觉因果中挖掘情感线索，缓解共现偏置，从而实现可解释的多模态情感理解。

Abstract: Emotional Video Captioning aims to generate factually accurate and emotionally empathetic descriptions. While recent methods have recognized the importance of visual causes to guide emotion perception and caption generation, they fundamentally rely on simple attention matching, which inevitably suffers from {causal redundancy and spurious correlations} in co-occurrence bias (e.g., misclassifying ``sadness'' as ``joy'' on a sunny beach), leading to severe shortcut learning from confusing backgrounds. Furthermore, existing evaluations fail to verify whether models have genuinely mastered causal reasoning or merely exploited background confounders. To address these limitations, we first construct {EVC-CauseGround}, a comprehensive benchmark with dense spatio-temporal causal annotations. Crucially, it introduces a carefully selected {Causal-Faithfulness Subset} to explicitly quantify genuine emotion-cause attribution. Second, we propose {Causal-EVC}, an emotion-grounding captioning framework, which introduces a Motion-guided Causal Spatiotemporal Localization module to precisely decouple causal triggers from background confounders. Besides, we introduce an Interpretable Sparse Emotion Routing module. By synthesizing counterfactual representations and formulating a novel counterfactual contrastive objective, we enforce the model to anchor its emotion predictions strictly on authentic causal triggers instead of confusing background. Extensive experiments show that Causal-EVC not only achieves the best performance on semantic metrics but also exhibits significant advantages in the causal-faithfulness subset, which demonstrates that our model could mine emotional cues from genuine visual causes and mitigate co-occurrence bias for interpretable multimodal emotion understanding.

</details>


### [62] [Decoding Affective Nuances: Enhancing MLLMs via Hierarchical Emotion Reasoning and Contrastive Discriminative Pruning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36782)
*Cheng Ye,Weidong Chen,Zhaobo Qi,Beier Zhu,Zhendong Mao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出免训练的推理期优化框架 DAN，通过分层情感推理链与对比判别视觉剪枝，提升多模态大模型对细粒度情感的区分能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大模型在客观理解任务上表现优异，但在情感推理上远逊于人类。其核心能力缺口在于难以基于细粒度视觉证据可靠区分语义相近的情感，具体表现为两点局限：1）归因不足，传统 MLLM 的全局推理范式稀释了细粒度情感线索，隐含编码的微妙情感状态导致复杂场景下误判；2）判别不足，现有方法只能定位与情感大致相关的区域，无法区分语义相似情感之间的判别性区域，造成情感判断模糊。

Method: 提出免训练、推理期优化的框架 Decoding Affective Nuances（DAN）。其一是分层情感推理链（HERC），通过协调细粒度的场景级与物体级线索并进行软门控推理，缓解归因不足问题；其二是对比判别视觉剪枝（CDVP），通过计算相似情感注意力分布之间的绝对差异，分离出具有判别性的视觉 token，以区分语义相近的情感并推断最终情感类别。

Result: 在多个基准上的实验表明，DAN 在不消耗额外训练资源的情况下显著提升了对情感细微差别的判别能力，尤其在包含 25 个细粒度情感类别的 WebEmo25 数据集上，搭载 Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct 时取得 +10.47% 的提升。

Conclusion: DAN 作为一种免训练的推理期优化框架，有效弥补了 MLLM 在细粒度情感归因与相似情感判别方面的不足，可即插即用地提升情感推理性能。

Abstract: While multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have demonstrated exceptional capabilities in objective understanding tasks, their performance in affective reasoning still falls significantly short of human standards. We attribute it to a central capability gap: MLLMs are difficult to reliably distinguish semantically proximal emotions based on fine-grained visual evidence, which could be decoupled as two limitations: 1) Insufficient Attribution. The global reasoning paradigm of conventional MLLMs severely dilutes fine-grained emotion cues, where subtle emotional states are usually implicitly encoded, thereby generating emotional misjudgments in complex scenarios. 2) Insufficient Discrimination. Existing methods could only identify regions generally associated with emotions, which fails to distinguish discriminative regions between semantically similar emotions, leading to ambiguous emotion judgements. To overcome these limitations, we present a training-free inference-time optimization framework, named Decoding Affective Nuances (DAN). Specifically, we propose a Hierarchical Emotional Reasoning Chain (HERC) that enhances the insufficient attribution by harmonizing fine-grained scene/object-level cues and performing a soft-gated reasoning. Furthermore, to discriminate between semantically proximal emotions, we design a Contrastive Discriminative Visual Pruning (CDVP), which isolates discriminative visual tokens to reason the final emotion category by computing the absolute discrepancy between the attention distributions of similar emotions. Performances on several benchmarks demonstrate that DAN significantly improves discrimination for affective nuances without consuming additional training resources, especially achieving +10.47% improvements with Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct on WebEmo25 dataset that contains 25 fine-grained emotion categories.

</details>


### [63] [Seeing What Should Be Heard: Diagnosing and Repairing Cross-Modal Shortcuts in Omni-Modal LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36798)
*Yueran Ma,Ronghao Lin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文揭示了全模态大模型普遍存在的跨模态捷径：回答音频问题时模型依赖图像的程度与音频相当甚至更高；作者提出诊断方法量化该现象，并提出DMC-Repair训练方法有效抑制捷径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全模态大模型被认为能按问题所指模态作答，但现有训练范式很少验证模型是否真正遵循该模态，因为同一样本的多模态输入往往为同一答案提供冗余证据，掩盖了跨模态捷径问题。

Method: 引入Factorized Modality Diagnostic，通过在样本间独立交换音频和图像来隔离各模态的因果贡献，从而系统诊断跨模态捷径；基于此提出DMC-Repair，在同类型跨模态交换样本上训练，并按问题指定的模态分配监督信号，防止模型利用同一片段内模态间的虚假对应关系。

Result: 在两个不同设置下的模型家族中发现该捷径贯穿监督微调和强化学习后训练，且基于评判的强化学习可能进一步放大对无关视觉信息的依赖；DMC-Repair将图像引起的答案效应占比降低59.9%，在不损害音频问答性能的前提下抑制捷径，且该效果可跨两个模型家族泛化，零样本迁移到未见数据集与基准，并在后续后训练中持续保持。

Conclusion: 全模态大模型中存在普遍的跨模态捷径，DMC-Repair能有效且可泛化地抑制该捷径而不牺牲音频问答性能，为多模态训练提供改进方向。

Abstract: Omni-modal large language models (LLMs) are expected to answer a question using the modality it explicitly refers to. However, existing training paradigms rarely verify whether models actually follow this modality, because multimodal inputs from the same sample often provide redundant evidence for the same answer. In this work, we uncover a pervasive cross-modal shortcut in omni-modal LLMs: when asked an audio-related question, models rely on the image as much as on the audio, and sometimes even more. To systematically diagnose this behavior, we introduce the Factorized Modality Diagnostic, which independently swaps audio and images between samples to isolate each modality's causal contribution. Across two model families in different settings, we find that this shortcut persists throughout supervised fine-tuning and reinforcement learning post-training, while judge-based RL may further amplify such reliance on irrelevant visual information. Based on this finding, we propose DMC-Repair, which trains models on the same kind of cross-modal swapped samples while assigning supervision according to the modality specified by the question. This prevents models from exploiting the spurious correspondence between modalities within the same clip. Experiments demonstrate that DMC-Repair reduces the image-induced share of the answer effect by 59.9%, effectively suppressing the cross-modal shortcut without compromising audio-question answering performance. The reduction in shortcut reliance generalizes across two model families and zero-shot to an unseen dataset and an unseen benchmark, and persists through subsequent post-training. Code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/DMC-Repair.

</details>


### [64] [Scene Retargeting: Learning Object Placement with Analogical Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36801)
*Minkwan Kim,Junho Kim,Seungmin Lee,Changwoon Choi,Young Min Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出"场景重定向"(Scene Retargeting)任务，通过聚类级迁移在布局间稳定传递语义连贯的空间组织，不依赖文本描述或成对关系，并在3D-FRONT数据集上超越现有布局生成方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交互式仿真（具身AI、空间计算）需要支持日常活动的真实3D场景，但稀疏、不规则的布局结构带来场景特定的物理约束，难以构建通用框架来生成功能相似的场景；现有方法依赖文本描述或成对关系，泛化能力有限。

Method: 将Scene Retargeting形式化为跨布局稳定迁移语义连贯的空间组织。采用聚类级(cluster-wise)迁移以灵活处理不匹配的物体实例并适应不同户型；通过尊重基础特征的空间分布来优化、保留各聚类丰富的语义上下文；再施加物理约束以优化墙面接触、成对对齐、通行空间与开口。

Result: 在3D-FRONT数据集的布局生成任务上，该框架优于当前最先进方法，并展示了真实到仿真迁移、类比轨迹迁移和多参考组合等下游应用。

Conclusion: 聚类级场景重定向提供了一种通用、可泛化的功能性场景布局迁移框架，能有效应对不规则布局与物理约束，具有广泛的下游应用价值。

Abstract: Interactive simulations of embodied AI or spatial computing applications build on realistic 3D scenes that support daily activities. However, sparse, irregular layout structures impose scene-specific physical constraints, making it hard to define a generalizable framework for generating similar functional context. We formalize Scene Retargeting as stably transferring the semantically coherent spatial organization across layouts, rather than relying on textual descriptions or pairwise relationships. Our cluster-wise transfer flexibly handles mismatched object instances and adapts to distinctive floor plans. We optimize to preserve the rich semantic context of individual clusters by respecting the spatial distribution of foundation features. We can then impose physical constraints to refine wall contacts, pairwise alignment, or clear passageways and openings. Our framework outperforms state-of-the-art methods on layout generation on the 3D-FRONT dataset, and demonstrates downstream applications including real-to-sim transfer, analogical trajectory transfer, and multi-reference composition.

</details>


### [65] [EGSD: Event-Grounded Self-Distillation for Streaming Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36803)
*Yuwei Miao,Xuesheng Zhang,Wenhao Zou,Jixia Zhang,Jianwei Lv,Bo Yuan,Junfeng Wang,Shiao Xie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出EGSD方法，通过事件驱动的自蒸馏解决流式视频理解中的记忆优化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实时视频理解需增量维护流式内容的记忆，而优化这一过程需要密集的过程信号。On-Policy Self-Distillation（OPSD）能提供token级信号，但直接应用于流式视频会带来两个问题：(1) 学生模型无法端到端优化，记忆在问题到来前就已写入，而教师却用问题与标准答案的特权来打分，造成偏好错位；(2) 有效实体记忆坍塌，问题与GT特权使教师只偏好与问题相关的实体，且token均值平均使信号对记忆覆盖实体数量不变，两者都使记忆偏离流式所需的多样性。

Method: 提出Event-Grounded Self-Distillation（EGSD），将流式记忆刻画为对可验证事件（关键视觉实体、动作、细节）的增量更新。针对问题(1)，将OPSD信号改造为与结果奖励相乘的乘性权重；针对问题(2)，用事件作为特权信息对教师重新加权以抵消其问题相关性偏差，并加入实体覆盖奖励以提供token均值教师所缺失的覆盖偏好。

Result: 在主流在线与离线基准上，EGSD取得强劲表现，StreamingBench达79.8%，OVO-Bench实时赛道达73.4%；记忆分析显示有效实体召回率提升17.4%，而记忆长度仅增加6.8%。

Conclusion: EGSD通过事件驱动的自蒸馏有效缓解了偏好错位与记忆坍塌问题，在提升流式视频理解性能的同时以极小记忆开销换取显著的有效实体覆盖提升。

Abstract: Real-time video understanding requires incrementally maintaining a memory of streaming content, and optimizing this requires dense process signals. On-Policy Self-Distillation (OPSD), which lets one model serve as both teacher and student with the teacher receiving additional privileged information such as the question and ground-truth (GT) answer, can supply such token-level signals. However, applying it directly to streaming video raises two problems. (1) The student cannot be optimized end-to-end, where memory is written before the question arrives, yet the teacher scores it with the question-and-GT privilege, misaligning their preferences. (2) Effective-entity memory collapses, where the question-and-GT privilege makes the teacher favor only question-relevant entities, and token-mean averaging over a memory renders its signal invariant to how many entities that memory covers, both driving memory against the streaming need for diversity. To address these issues, we propose Event-Grounded Self-Distillation (EGSD), which characterizes streaming memory as an incremental update over verifiable Events (key visual entities, actions, and details) and targets the two problems on this basis. For problem (1), we adapt the OPSD signal into a multiplicative weight combined with the outcome reward; for problem (2), we re-weight the teacher with Events as privileged information to counter its question-relevance bias, and add an entity-coverage reward to supply the coverage preference the token-mean teacher lacks. Extensive experiments on mainstream online and offline benchmarks show EGSD achieves strong performance, reaching 79.8% on StreamingBench and 73.4% on the OVO-Bench Real-Time track, while memory analysis shows effective-entity recall rises 17.4% at only 6.8% more memory length.

</details>


### [66] [MeteoVerse: Unified Weather-Controllable Video World Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36810)
*Renlong Wu,Guanqiao Wang,Xuan Shang,Yin Hanming,Xiaoxiao Sheng,Tianyu Huang,Hui Li,Wangmeng Zuo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 MeteoVerse，一种天气可控的视频世界模型，通过显式建模天气过渡并利用过渡感知的专家混合机制，统一天气的保留、引入与移除，实现精细天气控制并构建大规模数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实场景演化不仅受视角和物体动态影响，还受天气等环境条件影响；而天气变化所需修改取决于观测天气与目标天气的共同关系，可能需要保留、引入或移除天气效应。现有视频世界模型将天气过渡隐式处理，需生成骨干同时推断天气演化、场景动态与相机运动，导致天气控制不精确。

Method: 提出 MeteoVerse 统一天气可控视频世界模型：输入单张晴天或恶劣天气图像，条件为无天气场景描述、目标天气指令与相机轨迹，生成未来视频。显式估计观测天气状态与目标天气状态并表征所需天气过渡；采用过渡感知的天气专家混合模块，将过渡转化为类别特定的残差天气特征，统一天气保留、引入和移除，并支持对引入天气强度的细粒度控制。同时构建 MeteoVerse 数据集，包含超 5 万真实天气视频片段、生成的晴天对应版本、解耦的场景与天气描述、天气强度标注及相机轨迹。

Result: 大量实验表明，在保持有竞争力的场景一致性和相机控制性能的同时，天气可控性显著提升。

Conclusion: 显式建模天气过渡有助于提升视频世界模型的天气可控性，MeteoVerse 在天气控制与场景/相机一致性之间取得良好平衡。

Abstract: Video world models aim to predict future content from an observed scene while following prescribed camera motion. Real-world scene evolution is determined not only by changes in viewpoint and object dynamics, but also by environmental conditions such as weather, which can substantially alter scene appearance and visibility. Modeling such realistic weather evolution is challenging because the required weather modification depends jointly on the observed and desired weather states. Depending on their relation, the model may need to preserve, introduce, or remove a weather effect. Existing video world models typically leave this weather transition implicit, forcing the generation backbone to infer weather evolution together with scene dynamics and camera motion, which leads to imprecise weather control. To address this limitation, we propose MeteoVerse, a unified weather-controllable video world model that generates future videos from a single sunny or adverse-weather image, conditioned on a weather-free scene description, a target-weather instruction, and a camera trajectory. Rather than conditioning only on the desired weather, MeteoVerse explicitly estimates the observed and target weather states and represents the required weather transition. A transition-aware mixture of weather experts then translates this transition into category-specific residual weather features, unifying weather preservation, introduction, and removal while enabling fine-grained control over introduced weather intensity. We further construct the MeteoVerse dataset with over 50K real-world weather video clips, generated sunny counterparts, disentangled scene and weather descriptions, weather-intensity annotations, and camera trajectories. Extensive experiments demonstrate substantially improved weather controllability while retaining competitive scene consistency and camera-control performance.

</details>


### [67] [CurvSpec: Adaptive Multi-Curvature Learning for Partial Relevant Video Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36815)
*Zhen Liu,Letian Li,Jinpeng Wang,Shuzhao Xie,Yuzhi Huang,Jingyan Jiang,Zhi Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 CurvSpec，通过学习内容自适应曲率并结合语义质心表示，缓解 PRVR 中的信号稀释与曲率刚性问题，在三个数据集上取得最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部分相关视频检索（PRVR）需在无时间标注下检索含相关片段的未剪辑视频，相关时刻可能仅数秒而视频长达数分钟，信噪比极低。现有方法面临两大交织挑战：一是信号稀释，粗粒度全局表示将短暂相关信号淹没在大量无关内容中；二是曲率刚性，将所有视频嵌入同一固定几何空间，难以建模从扁平原子事件到深层组合结构的多样视频结构。

Method: 提出 CurvSpec 框架，学习内容自适应曲率而非固定几何先验。特征经并行的欧几里得与双曲注意力层处理，双曲层赋予独立学习的曲率，并通过内容感知融合机制为每个输入选择最合适的几何区域。为进一步抑制信号稀释，用语义质心表示视频，质心数量由视频内容复杂度决定，并将其投影到学习到的流形上，通过测地距离将查询与最近质心匹配。

Result: 在 ActivityNet Captions、TVR 和 Charades-STA 三个数据集上的实验表明该方法达到当前最优（state-of-the-art）检索性能。

Conclusion: 通过内容自适应曲率与质心式视频表示，CurvSpec 有效应对 PRVR 的信号稀释与曲率刚性问题，验证了自适应几何建模在部分相关视频检索中的有效性与优越性。

Abstract: Partially Relevant Video Retrieval (PRVR) seeks to retrieve untrim-med videos containing a moment that matches a text query, without temporal annotations. The relevant moment may last only seconds within a video spanning several minutes, creating an extremely low signal-to-noise ratio that makes PRVR more challenging than standard full-video retrieval. This task presents two intertwined challenges: (1) signal dilution, where coarse global representations blur the brief relevant signal into the dominant irrelevant surroundings;(2) curvature rigidity, where embedding all videos in the same fixed-geometry space distorts representations for videos that range from flat atomic events to deep compositional hierarchies. Existing PRVR methods have improved moment selection and cross-modal matching, but they still typically encode all videos in a single fixed-curvature retrieval space, limiting their ability to model diverse video structures. To address both challenges, we propose CurvSpec, a framework that learns content-adaptive curvature for video retrieval representations rather than imposing a fixed geometric prior. CurvSpec processes features through parallel Euclidean and hyperbolic attention layers, with independently learned curvatures assigned to the hyperbolic layers, and a content-aware fusion mechanism routes each input to its most suitable geometric regime. To further suppress signal dilution, CurvSpec represents each video with semantic centroids whose number is determined by the video's content complexity, projects them onto the learned manifold, and matches each query against its nearest centroid by geodesic distance. Experiments on ActivityNet Captions, TVR, and Charades-STA demonstrate state-of-the-art retrieval performance.

</details>


### [68] [RED: Reconstruction Evolution Dynamics for Generalizable AI-Generated Image Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36822)
*Wenpeng Mu,Junshan Jin,Tanfeng Sun,Xinghao Jiang,Qiang Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出RED框架，利用多尺度重建过程中token可预测性的变化来检测AI生成图像，在六个基准上取得92.5%平均准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图像生成器快速演化，需要能泛化到未知生成机制的取证线索；现有检测器依赖静态图像表示或终点重建差异，忽视了中间重建阶段的演化信息。作者观察到真实与生成图像的相对token可预测性会在不同重建尺度上发生反转，提示中间阶段可能暴露被终点比较忽略的取证证据。

Method: 提出RED框架，从粗到细的重建演化中捕获可迁移取证线索。用冻结的多尺度VQ-VAE生成重建轨迹，并映射到冻结CLIP编码器的共享特征空间；利用冻结VAR模型提供的尺度级token负对数似然，学习图像自适应的阶段权重，将可预测性变化与视觉证据关联；再通过跨阶段证据聚合模块联合建模原图表示与加权重建特征，沿重建轨迹捕获互补线索。

Result: 在六个多样化基准上，RED取得最高平均准确率92.5%和平均精度97.5%；对常见图像退化表现出强鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 重建演化过程为可泛化的AI生成图像检测提供了有价值的信息，RED验证了利用中间重建阶段取证线索的有效性，代码将在论文接收后公开。

Abstract: The rapid evolution of image generators calls for forensic cues that generalize beyond known generation mechanisms. Existing detectors often rely on static image representations or endpoint reconstruction discrepancies, leaving the evolution of intermediate reconstruction stages underexplored.
